- **verify**: Self-check your own completed change before independent review — the pre-review sanity pass. Use when you want to check your work, run checks, validate changes, make sure a change is ready, test it end-to-end, run repo guardrails (lint, typecheck, tests, build), exercise the real surface with evidence, and catch obvious self-correctable issues.
